在工单06之前插入工单06A，输出CUFixture.app与事件记录器。按conformance的AppKit fixture规范创建固定identifier控件和可控状态，不以TextEdit/真实浏览器账户替代。测试窗口显示按钮counter、文本框当前value、focus owner、最后事件坐标/key/modifier、window ID、事件序号；按钮可销毁重建同role/title元素、制造重复descriptor、隐藏/禁用控件、切窗口、最小化。

事件记录器使用monotonic时间、target PID/window、action count和最终状态生成JSON，不记录真实用户数据。后台观察器窗口保持焦点并接收测试键串，用于测目标动作是否抢焦点。无AX场景用自有画布显示合成文字和已知bbox；browser fixture只用本地静态页面，不访问真实账户。fixture的构建/启动也是需要真实执行的验收，不是文档内已完成成果。

## 安全模式的固定设计常量

以下值是为了减少后续实现选择而固定的独立设计，不是逆向发现的Alma常量。初始配置写入contracts/safe-policy.json：maxNativeFrameBytes=8388608（8MiB）；maxImageBytes=16777216（16MiB）；maxImagesPerMessage=4；maxActiveThreadImageBytes=134217728（128MiB）；maxTotalImageCacheBytes=1073741824（1GiB）；maxTextLogValueChars=0（默认不记输入文本）；approvalTimeoutMs=5000；defaultRpcTimeoutMs=20000；captureRpcTimeoutMs=30000；maxConcurrentActionsPerTarget=1；liveInputEnabled=false。所有上限超出都返回明确错误，不截掉JSON/图片后当成功。

安全模式用full SHA256作图片asset identity，临时写入后原子rename。cache预算不足先清无持久消息引用的最旧资产，仍不足则拒绝新写并返回replica.cache_full；不得删活跃消息资产或让mapping指向别图。处理文本日志只记长度、可选不可逆摘要和结果，不记正文。生产权限/隐私需求改变这些值时，更新decision和测试，不在实现中散放magic number。

请求在validation/resolve/authorize前取消，outcome=not_started；已进入dispatch但无完成证据则unknown；调用返回只证明接受时为effect_unverified；只有fixture或后续状态明确证实时verified。默认绝不输出“已停止所有动作”。safe_profile的错误code以replica.开头；原有code/hint作为可选cause保留。取消不回滚、不自动重放。兼容模式保留原包装的无signal语义，但只在隔离测试使用。

## 可执行依赖图与临时实现边界

依赖顺序并不是简单把文件按数字实现。实际DAG固定为：00→01→02→03→03B→04→05；00→06A→06；06→07→08→09→10；06→14A；07→11A；09+14A→12；07+14A→13；11A+12+13→11B；04+05+11B→15；09+15→16；14A+16→17；06A+09+15→18；全部→19。

14A分两段：先实现PermissionService、FocusGuard mock/lease与健康状态、目标绑定、down/up释放finally；再经授权运行只读event tap健康和前台旁路观察器测试，不注入输入。第二段成功且fixture隔离门槛通过后，只允许test-scoped live input，未验证时保持关闭。14B是执行动作后的焦点压力验收，不阻塞此前已通过的只读健康门。11A只实现AX纯动作与mock fallback，缺PointerDispatcher时必须返回replica.capability_unavailable，不能先调用全局鼠标。11B在12/13通过后才接真实fallback。14的高级焦点验收可继续完善，但不能把安全门槛留到已经执行指针之后。03–05全用fake daemon和fakeHTTP，不需要TCC。

每张工单exit还必须包含依赖工单状态；dependency不是passed就不得宣称当前live capability可用。允许纯mock代码编译推进，但状态写implemented_mock，不写runtime_verified。

## 统一类型边界

这些是独立实现的内部类型选择。对外仍按各入口契约序列化，尤其不能把内部更丰富的错误字段偷偷当成 Alma 已证输出。

JSONValue = null | boolean | number | string | JSONValue[] | {[key:string]:JSONValue}。编码器不得输出 NaN/Infinity；compatibility 的 CLI 转型应单独测量其转成 JSON null 的边角，不能让内部数值类型被污染。

RequestEnvelope = {id:string, cmd:string, args:object}是独立实现内部规范化后的类型；原生wire允许args省略或null，decoder应先按原契约接受，再在内部规范化，不可用该内部类型拒绝原输入。客户端正常生成 16 位 hex id；服务端是否严格限制该格式必须作为独立策略，而非假定原产品已校验。每帧为 UTF-8 JSON 加 LF。ResponseEnvelope 为 {id:string, ok:boolean, data?:JSONValue, error?:NativeError} 的内部表示；原产品真实错误字段位置以 native 命令附录为准，适配层必须逐字段对照，不能靠这个内部类型推断。

NativeError = {code:string, message:string, hint?:string, phase?:string, outcome?:string}。后两个字段属于安全实现的扩展：phase ∈ validate/resolve/authorize/observe/dispatch/postobserve；outcome ∈ not_started/accepted/effect_unverified/verified/unknown。没有原样错误码证据时，在兼容入口不要编造 Alma error code；在自有入口用明确命名空间 replica.*。

TargetRequest = {bundle?:string, pid?:integer, windowId?:integer, ref?:string}。ResolvedTarget = {pid:integer, bundle?:string, windowId?:integer, source:bundle|pid|ref, evidenceGeneration?:string}。safe profile 一次解析后把同一个 ResolvedTarget 交给批准、动作、截图和日志；目标冲突直接返回 replica.target_mismatch，且不得执行动作。compatibility profile 保留各包装已证的目标拆分，限测试使用。

SnapshotKey = {pid:integer, windowId:integer|null}；序列化为十进制 PID、冒号、十进制 windowId 或减号。ElementRef 为不透明字符串，禁止调用者解析其中计数推断有效性。ElementDescriptor 包含 role、可选 subrole/title/identifier/roleDescription、parentRoles。ElementEntry 包含 live AX handle、snapshotId、pid、可选 descriptor、invalidated。

ElementRecord 内部至少包含 ref、role，以及可选 subrole/title/value/description/role_description/enabled/focused/children_count/actions/bounds。bounds 的单位必须带类型标识 ScreenPointRect 或 ScreenshotPixelRect；禁止通用 CGRect 在边界处无单位传递。完整原生输出的可选字段与 Swift 的字符串化行为要通过命令契约和 golden fixtures 校准。

ScreenshotArtifact = {path:string, pixelWidth:integer, pixelHeight:integer, capturedRectPoints:Rect, target:ResolvedTarget, capturedAtMonotonicMs:number}。内部路径和 mapping 必须一同提交；图片写失败不能更新映射。外部 screenshot.path、data_url、url 三个字段绝不互相假定等价。普通 MCP 读 path；sky 状态优先 data_url 再 url；宿主/CLI另按契约处理。

CancellationToken = {requested:boolean, reason?:string}；ActionReceipt = {accepted:boolean, delivery?:boolean, outcome:enum, method?:string}。token 只是协作取消信号，不是停止 OS 已排队事件的证明。进入 dispatch 后失去响应时使用 unknown；禁止自动重放 click/type/drag/press/set_value。

## 接口签名与所有权

以下签名固定模块责任；不是要求采用某个未经验证的第三方库。

Transport.connect() -> Connection；Transport.call(command,args,deadline) -> Promise<NativeResult>；Transport.close() -> void。只有 transport 能写 socket 和修改 pending。单元测试注入 ByteStream 与 MonotonicClock；禁止直接睡眠等待来测边界。

TargetResolver.resolve(request,mode) -> ResolvedTarget。输入字段的 schema 校验在 adapter；OS 存活性、bundle 到 PID 和窗口归属检查在 resolver。ref 解引用只有 ElementStore 能做。若 ref 所属 PID 已退出，不能退化成全局前台操作。

PermissionService.inspect() -> PermissionSnapshot；PermissionService.request(kind) -> PermissionRequestReceipt。授权请求返回不代表已授予；随后必须再 inspect。不要把 UI 批准记录复用为 TCC 状态。

ElementStore.beginSnapshot() -> SnapshotId；register(axElement,pid,descriptor) -> ElementRef；resolve(ref) -> Entry|Failure；clear() -> void。store 拥有 observer/run loop source 生命周期。SnapshotBuilder 只能通过 store 注册，不能自己生成 eN。实际调用 AX 的线程/主 run loop 安排必须通过 macOS fixture 验证。

SnapshotBuilder.capture(target,options) -> FullSnapshot；StateDiff.apply(key,fullElements,disabled) -> DiffResult。先获得 fullElements，再在 diff 层产生 changed/removed/unchanged；不要直接遍历时构造差分，因为删除项需要对照完整新 ref 集。

CaptureService.captureWindow(target,options) -> ScreenshotArtifact；captureDisplay(display,options) -> ScreenshotArtifact。CoordinateMapper.commit(artifact) 原子更新 mapping；toScreen(pid,pixelPoint) -> ScreenPoint|NoMapping。compatibility 用 PID key；safe 推荐使用 snapshot/window 绑定的 mapping id，并要求动作引用对应 mapping，作为明确协议扩展。

AXActions.click(entry,options) -> ActionReceipt；PointerDispatcher.click(target,screenPoint,options) -> ActionReceipt；KeyboardDispatcher.press(target,keySpec) -> ActionReceipt。三者不得悄悄激活目标 app；如某能力只能靠激活实现，需单独能力/明确允许，不能作为后台路径的隐藏回退。

FocusGuard.begin(scope) -> GuardLease；GuardLease.close() -> void；FocusGuard.health() -> Health。close 必须可重复调用。arm 失败是可观测结果，不能吞掉并声称零焦点抢占。健康警告的缓存只属于包装展示，不改变 guard 真正状态。

PipStack.present(item,automatic) -> PresentResult；dismiss(id) -> void；invalidate(id) -> void；hideProgrammatically() -> void。PipStack 不采图；FrameSource 只采图；Renderer 只展示。每个资源 lease 都有 owner id 与 generation，safe profile 用 generation 忽略旧异步完成。

AppshotController.arm(hotkey|null) -> State；captureNow() -> CaptureReceipt；Composer.consume(token,capture) -> void。capture 文件、动画、IPC、附件处理分别回报失败；不能把显示动画当作附件已就绪，也不能自动发送用户消息。

## 依赖顺序工单

每张工单只有在 exit 条件可展示时才结束。下列步骤不是估时，禁止因“编译通过”跳过后续的行为验证。附带 conformance 目录提供 fixture 与用例；把当前工单对应要求 IDs 写进提交说明。

### 工单 01 固定证据与兼容表

输入：样本 manifest、组件 hash、工具/CLI/native/HTTP 契约、审计报告。输出：docs/decisions/0001-scope.md 与 compatibility.json。逐项登记原行为、证据定位、safe 差异、未决点。建立 requirements 的实现文件链接，暂不写 OS 操作代码。exit：所有公共工具、CLI命令、HTTP route 都有唯一 owner；未闭合项有 STOP 状态。

### 工单 02 JSON 值与分帧

仅修改 Protocol/Envelope.swift、JSONLFramer.swift、测试。按字节累积，只有遇 LF 才解码完整行；同一 read 可以产生多帧，单帧也可以跨 read。测试跨 UTF-8 多字节字符拆分，避免把分片先转 String 导致替换字符。safe profile 设最大帧限制并在重连清空 buffer；compatibility 记录原客户端未见限制/未清空的差异。exit：分片、合并、空行、坏JSON、无LF、超限测试都有结果。

### 工单 03 客户端状态机

仅实现 daemonClient 和 fake daemon。状态为 disconnected/connecting/connected/closing；并发 connect 共用 promise；pending 按 id 管理。默认20s，显式状态/补图30s，连接等待与请求计时分开。late/unknown id 忽略；断连拒绝全部pending；请求超时不重发。exit：虚拟时钟能逐 tick 证明只 resolve/reject 一次；取消时不会自动构造 native cancel。

### 工单 03B 守护进程路由与 Ping

依赖02和03。在自有CUDaemon实现AF_UNIX listener、连接context、RequestRouter白名单与纯ping/diagnostic handler；此时不注册真实输入handler。连接任务顺序与并发行为按原native章，在safe_profile加帧长限制与独立connection epoch。ping返回独立产品名、版本、能力与测试profile，不冒充Alma。用03的客户端对自有临时socket做ping round-trip，再测坏JSON、半行、两peer、断连/短写/清理；这些通过才进入04。exit：请求id一一对应，错误无副作用，server可正常退出并只删自己socket。

### 工单 04 纯工具包装

接 mock daemon 与 fake HTTP，不接 macOS。逐个照 tool-inputs 和 payload_contract 写 handler，保留普通/sky 不同默认、schema上限、gate、图片格式、summary语义。不要先写通用 normalizeEverything 再套所有工具。exit：所有23工具有合法/省略/错误/边界测试；ref-only 不补图、window-only 不补图、sky path-only 无图、delivered=false非异常均可复现。

### 工单 05 本地 HTTP 和 CLI

先 route mock，再 CLI。按 cli-command-catalog 区分位置参数、命令别名与共同预解析。--json 不是统一原样输出模式。safe profile 的严格数值输入单独显式记录。exit：22 canonical 功能命令、help 与 launch别名均覆盖；每条调用顺序和退出码可由mock断言。

### 工单 06 测试应用与权限诊断

先建立合成 AppKit/浏览器 fixture，再接真实系统。界面元素有稳定标识、可控制的销毁/重建、重复标题、滚动区、菜单快捷键、无AX画布和可见事件计数。PermissionService 先只读状态，再通过人工批准步骤验证 request。exit：测试 app可以报告前台/按键/鼠标/焦点/文本变化，且拒绝权限时无静默成功。

### 工单 07 AX 存储与恢复

以 mock AXHandle 的稳定 identity 先测 beginSnapshot、register 和 descriptor 搜索，再接AX对象。ref在同PID+CFEqual且当前snapshot尚未使用时复用；销毁/无效分支按native章实现。恢复必须有200节点、12层、最近3祖先role与唯一候选限制。exit：零候选和多候选都失败；禁止选择第一个看起来相似的元素。

### 工单 08 AX 快照和差分

先做全量输出再做 diff。独立纯函数实现固定字段指纹、bounds整像素取整、PID/window key、removed_refs排序。禁用diff仍更新基线。exit：新增/修改/删除/不变/窗口切换/禁用重置/subpixel边界都有golden结果；sky如何忽略删除另在adapter测试，不能改native来“补救”。

### 工单 09 截图与坐标

依次完成 window shot、display shot、JPEG写盘、mapping发布。用明确point与pixel类型；公式见native章。先用合成矩形测，再跑1×/2×/混合DPI/负坐标屏幕。exit：针对每张截图的已知标记，转换落点在容差内；发生窗口切换或mapping不存在时不静默用屏幕坐标。

### 工单 10 OCR 状态补充

按最终native章的触发门槛与VisualIndex契约接Vision；只在证据明确的内部路径触发，不凭空注册公开ocr工具。OCR ref 与 AX ref 路径分开，不对视觉框执行AX set_value。exit：空、单节点、多个AX元素和无文字图像的门槛用例明确；OCR边框原点/缩放/最大结果数符合测试。

### 工单 11 AX 动作

先set_value/select_text，再ref点击/secondary/scroll。每项检查角色/支持action/目标PID，按原native章决定AX路径和物理回退。返回accepted与effect分开。exit：fixture记录目标控件改变，失败仍能区分no_ref、stale、unsupported、system_error；不会把失败回退到当前前台app。

### 工单 12 指针输入

严格按native静态算法与已验证SPI前提实现。safe_profile要求每次down有清理路径尝试up；原alma_0_4_164拖拽最终up仍可能创建失败且未证无条件补偿，兼容用例应记录该结果而不是悄悄套修复。拖拽插值使用明确时间和坐标。show_cursor控制显示层并不等于不动硬件指针，需实测分开记录。exit：后台/遮挡/目标退出/中途取消/多按钮/拖拽异常不会留下卡键卡鼠标；无法验证的私有字段不填猜值。

### 工单 13 键盘与文本

先key parser与modifier规范化纯测试，再菜单快捷键AXPress，再前台/可投递路径；Unicode事件按最多20个Unicode scalar分块；select_text/AX range使用UTF-16单位；布局翻译按完整Swift Character匹配。三种计数不能混用，emoji不得拆代理对。不得用剪贴板替代未证实的输入机制。exit：emoji/组合字符/CJK、前台与后台、菜单命中/禁用、delivered=false都被fixture真实记录；包装摘要与实际效果分别断言。

### 工单 14 焦点防护

实现guard lease、事件tap健康状态与temporary activation allowance；以主动前台测试app持续输入作为旁路观察器。exit：成功、arm失败、tap禁用/重启均有证据；报告记录焦点变化次数，无法确保时标能力受限，不能只看showInactive就判通过。

### 工单 15 宿主模型输出与停止

接MCP client、toModelOutput、图片落盘/再水合和watchdog。compatibility呈现原isError损失；safe保留结构化失败。外层停止、MCP取消、socket断开和native事件继续是四个独立观测量。exit：文本模型/视觉模型、坏图片、图片读失败、watchdog超时、迟到响应都有明确结果；不再用terminated作无依据保证。

### 工单 16 被动镜像

先只用合成frame source完成stack，再接computerPump/CDP lease。固定关闭抑制键、滑动600000ms、420ms删除、90s活动与333ms/220ms双循环。exit：持续活动超过10分钟不意外重开、手动打开清抑制、source切换/失败/在途关闭无资源泄漏。原竞态在compatibility中是待观察行为，safe用generation修正。

### 工单 17 Lens 与原生 PiP

将其当成单独组件，不把Electron镜像重新命名成native PiP。Lens默认、ActionCursor记录时点、窗口流硬件cursor行为都按native展示报告。exit：关闭和释放只影响所属lease；模拟光标与硬件cursor分别可测；关闭设置不被误当成禁止系统事件。

### 工单 18 Appshot

实现arm/disarm、capturingNow、request id、target选择、1100ms动画与715ms发送、消费token、AX上下文迟到、附件失败。这里允许的显式聚焦Alma行为与被动镜像不同。exit：重复热键只产生一个有效捕获、附件不会自动发送、早到/迟到context规则清楚，授权失败不会假称active。

### 工单 19 全链闭环

执行conformance staged plan，从合成工具输入到fixture效果再到模型可见结果留同一correlation id。每个已承诺能力均有证据；每个失败均能落到已知层；所有P0安全门槛通过后才允许真实工作流试用。exit：形成一份按OS版本、架构、显示器配置、权限身份记录的运行报告，而不是只展示一次成功视频。

## 模型执行纪律与停止条件

每次给执行模型只交付当前工单、所需契约、相关算法、对应fixtures和上一步结果。提示它先复述字段优先级与已知未知，再写最小实现；复述不一致时先纠正，不允许一口气生成全项目。

每次提交必须附：改动文件列表、对应requirement/test IDs、已运行命令与真实输出摘要、未运行测试、profile、安全偏离、下一步。没有运行环境时只标“未运行”；不要用“应当通过”替代结果。

遇到下列情况停止该工单并输出阻塞记录：证据互相冲突；native分支来源不明；缺少私有SPI签名或字段含义；没有macOS权限/签名身份；无法确定坐标单位；测试需真实私密数据；需要用户没有授权的外部通信/安装/权限更改；代码无法证明事件释放。可以继续独立的纯函数工单，但不能在未知处编一个行为再宣称兼容。

阻塞记录格式固定为：requirement_id、evidence_used、missing_fact、why_it_changes_behavior、safe_temporary_behavior、next_observation、owner、status。临时行为默认拒绝该能力并给出可诊断错误，而不是静默降级到更广目标。

未知项被补证后，先更新契约和fixture，再改实现。不要把一次运行结果覆盖静态已证的所有分支；一个fixture只证明其OS/目标app/权限/显示器条件下的行为。

## 最常见的复刻错误

- 仅把23个MCP函数做出来，忽略HTTP/CLI另一条路径和native状态
- 把bundle、PID、ref、window_id当成四种等价目标
- 用截图宽度除屏幕宽度代替当前截图mapping
- 每次snapshot清空ref表，使sky索引与后续操作失效
- 把diff元素列表当全量树，或者遗漏removed_refs和基线reset
- 自动重试超时click/type，引发重复输入
- 把收到取消通知等同于系统事件已停止
- 只看HTTP200、MCP isError缺省、日志✓或CLI成功句判断效果
- 把OCR类名、Lens类名或PIPSession符号的存在当成每次调用实际经过该路径
- 把show_cursor=false、Lens off、没有显示光标、硬件指针未移动混为一谈
- 使用无真实前台旁路监测的单次录像证明绝不抢焦点
- 将整屏截图回退隐藏在成功结果中，没有注明可能扩大可见范围
- 镜像关闭只销毁窗口，不取消或隔离采集中的晚完成
- 将Appshot文本作为高优先级指令，或捕获后自动发送聊天


# 合成测试与 macOS 验收


## 这份包证明什么

本包是原创测试设计，不是 Alma 的测试结果，也不是可运行的产品实现。只运行我们编写的数据生成器和通用 JSON Schema 校验器，没有执行提取的 JavaScript、CLI、Mach-O、应用或其服务。`STATIC_EXPECTATION` 表示从静态证据导出的可测试预期，不表示该测试已通过。`UNKNOWN_REQUIRE_RUNTIME` 是阻塞结论的占位值，绝不能当作宽松通配符使测试通过。

本包覆盖静态材料已知的外部边界，不声称穷尽 macOS 的系统状态、私有 SPI 或所有原生算法。每个精确常量来自证据；fixture 自选尺寸、等待预算和像素容差是测试设计，不能反写为 Alma 默认值。

## 文件与执行顺序

先读 `P0.zh-CN.md` 和 `p0-critical-subset.json` 的39项关键检查，再按模块展开；总734条分为453条schema排列、113条逐项编写契约、168个runtime矩阵格。

1. `evidence-registry.json`：证据 ID 与原始定位文件/分析章节
2. `requirements-traceability.json`：11 类要求→case IDs→evidence IDs
3. `contract-vectors.json`：合成输入与结构化预期，普通接口/sky接口/安全版本分别断言
4. `contract-vectors.schema.json`：向量自身的格式 schema
5. `fixture-specifications.json`：受控 AppKit、Electron、浏览器夹具与旁路见证者
6. `runtime-result.schema.json`：将来真实运行必须填写的结果格式
7. `staged-plan.json`：阶段进入/退出条件
8. `module-handoffs.json`：单模块编码模型工单；与工程手册固定路径对应
9. `validate_conformance.py`：只校验我们自己的资料，不启动目标
10. `validation-report.json`：本次资料校验结果，不是产品测试通过报告

工程架构、文件地图、模块接口和19张总工单，以 `chapters/08_独立实现工程手册.md` 为准。本包不另造第二套架构。对外分发时该章会成为主文档一部分。

## 严格判定规则

- 先选择 `alma_0_4_164`（对应工程手册 compatibility）或 `safe_profile`（对应 safe），测试开始后不得换 profile
- oracle.expected 是需要逐项断言的观测投影，不是整个工具结果的字节快照。例如 `native_args_forbidden_keys` 检查这些键缺失；`summary_contains` 做子串检查；`image_blocks` 统计 MCP image block；`json_screenshot_key_present` 检查解码后的状态正文，不包括其他 text block
- 字符串 error_class 如 TARGET_MISMATCH 是测试分类，不是推断出的 Alma 错误码；安全实现把它映射为自身 `replica.*` 错误码。额外字段未被断言时不得默认为不存在
- `schema_valid:true` 只表示输入 schema 接受；不保证 handler 必要参数检查通过，不保证 ref有效，更不保证事件发生。例如 click最小对象通过字段schema，handler随后仍可拒绝
- `null` 与省略不同。原 schema 转录未断言 unknown-property stripping 行为，本包不补猜。NaN/Infinity不是JSON，不应伪装成JSON向量
- 向量中的 fixture:// 路径由 mock filesystem 解析，禁止当真URL或真实磁盘路径读取。010203故意不是JPEG：用于证明原包装不校验图片内容；图像质量测试使用夹具生成的有效JPEG
- 同一用例中多个可异步排序事件，应按规定偏序断言；只有已证顺序才能要求全序。未知动态竞态用运行证据闭合，不能凭mock选择一个结果
- runtime未知项即使采到了截图也仍为 INCONCLUSIVE，直到接收端、焦点、指针、最终状态和原始响应相互吻合

## 分层 oracle，禁止“看起来成功”

L0：输入是否被schema接受。L1：包装究竟发送了什么cmd/args、向谁询问批准、是否补图。L2：daemon是否接受、回复、超时、断线。L3：接收端收到什么及真实状态变化。L4：前台用户焦点/键盘/真实鼠标是否受影响。L5：UI和模型是否准确呈现L2/L3的结果。每层单独报告。

不允许以下推理：有 `ok:true` 所以点击有效；MCP没有isError所以按键送达；CLI打印成功所以输入完成；UI有✓所以事件有效；显示新截图所以ref未失效；cancel返回所以native停止；showInactive存在所以绝无瞬时焦点变化；native配置showsCursor=false所以所有capture路径都无硬件鼠标。

## 无重放约束

所有profile均须记录每个mutation的dispatch次数。超时、取消、断线、图片失败后，禁止自动再次发送click/type/drag/press/scroll/set_value等变更。测试控制器也不得因为结果未知而重试同一次动作。若需重跑，先停止/确认旧请求结局、创建新run_id、复位夹具、记录独立op_id，并将其记为新实验。截图失败只重做经明确授权的只读观察，不把原动作一并重跑。

## 受控环境与权限

未来动态阶段仅对独立测试应用、合成文本、合成文件和专用测试桌面执行。TCC授予、系统权限修改、锁屏/解锁、私有SPI实测需要相应用户授权；fixture清单不构成授权。不得操控真实账户、支付、聊天输入框或密码窗口，不得将用户桌面作为可分享的截图样本。

锁屏、protected media、Secure Input、不支持的系统版本、缺权限均为独立能力条件；不得绕过保护来“修好”测试。safe版本在不能证明安全/目标身份时返回明确不可用且不投递。compatibility对原样行为未知时记为UNKNOWN，不能复制假想的绕过策略。

## Runtime-01—12验收规则

1. RUNTIME-01 身份：run_id/op_id、profile、样本hash或复刻commit、macOSbuild、架构、屏幕布局、权限、fixture版本齐全；缺任一关键项为INCONCLUSIVE
2. RUNTIME-02 接收：在已声明支持的场景，只有预期控件产生一次预期逻辑状态变化；无关控件/旁路前台app无变更。AX动作可以没有mouseDown，但必须有匹配控件状态/AX action见证
3. RUNTIME-03 无重放：每个op_id只派发一次变更；timeout/cancel/截图失败后dispatch仍为1或未启动时0，不得大于1
4. RUNTIME-04 输入完整性：文本按Unicode标量与原始UTF-16两种方式存档；普通文本框最终字符串必须与指定插入/替换语义精确一致。组合输入/IME另列，不把规范等价默认为字节相等
5. RUNTIME-05 释放：正常和异常路径结束后，测试记录没有未配对的已观察key/button down；观察器不可见或进程崩溃造成缺测时为INCONCLUSIVE，不能判通过
6. RUNTIME-06 焦点：被动后台能力声称不抢焦点时，前台bundle/window序列与焦点通知中不得有目标/镜像瞬时激活，旁路输入接收完整。只有轮询无事件日志不能证明零瞬时切换；报告采样盲区
7. RUNTIME-07 光标：hardware cursor与ActionCursor/Lens三条轨迹独立存档；无物理鼠标移动能力只有在硬件轨迹未变化且测量覆盖时通过。show_cursor=false不是其替代断言
8. RUNTIME-08 截图：新capture_id文件可解码、尺寸与元数据一致、不是前一代图片；四角标记/中心标记落在测试选定容差内（几何误差≤1 screenshot pixel，JPEG颜色不要求逐字节相等）
9. RUNTIME-09 隐私：safe窗口截图不得在失败时悄悄切到全桌面；若启用桌面回退需明示范围。原mirror窗口失败→display fallback只在compatibility隔离夹具中验证
10. RUNTIME-10 取消：dispatch前取消不得有接收端变更；dispatch后允许已执行部分，但必须完整记录、释放输入、标明实际或未知结局，不得无证据声明回滚/终止
11. RUNTIME-11 资源：关闭后没有新的所属轮询/流租约，旧generation的迟到完成不能重新开窗或发布帧；原静态已知竞态在compatibility中标观察结果，不偷偷套安全版本要求
12. RUNTIME-12 能力矩阵：每个系统/场景独立列supported/unsupported/unverified；只有满足该场景全部相关oracle才可宣称支持。默认可见窗口通过不外推到遮挡、最小化、锁屏或多屏

## 已知阻塞点

- 真实窗口遮挡/最小化/锁屏和跨Spaces可用性；系统版本与私有事件投递的稳定性
- native队列、socket断开、取消、shutdown时已排队事件的结局
- OCR触发已确认：成功截图且原始snapshot.elements.count≤1；不是diff结果数。原生命令字面量为snap，普通MCP动作补图为get_app_state(depth=0)，两者不可混同。仍需运行验证OCR坐标与系统行为
- diff的Swift字段文本化及±0.5等边界应有golden验证；本包刻意避免伪造完整AX输出
- ElementStore销毁通知和PID退出的最终清理；refetch对动态树的竞态
- 原host图像到每种provider后的实际能力处理；isError丢失后的UI显示
- mirror在途退出、陈旧frame、跨display切换与native独立PiP资源归属

这些项目不是“剩余小修”。在被选为交付能力时，它们是发布门槛。

## 解压后独立校验（不需要原始安装包）

在分发包根目录运行：

`python3 analysis/conformance/validate_conformance.py`

依赖为Python 3及`jsonschema==4.26.0`（见本目录requirements.txt）。该依赖是通用schema校验工具；当前研究环境已有该版本，没有为此安装软件。接收者如尚未安装，可在自己的独立虚拟环境按requirements.txt安装。默认命令只读取分发包里的JSON、schema、证据索引和报告，不读取原始Alma文件，不执行Alma，也不会自动下载任何文件。

只有持有自己授权取得的原始研究样本且明确想复核源区间hash时，才增加：

`python3 analysis/conformance/validate_conformance.py --sample-root /path/to/research-root`

该目录需包含source-locators所指的extracted/路径；仍然只读字节，不执行源代码。默认报告将sample_hash_verification标为SKIPPED_NO_SAMPLE_REQUESTED，此状态不影响资料一致性通过。`portable-validation-report.json` 是去掉所有原始样本后在独立临时副本运行的真实资料校验结果；产品测试数仍为0。

P0新增视觉ref阻断：普通MCP click会丢弃v*路径需要的bundle/PID，native在查visual cache之前返回invalid_arg；sky状态也不把visual_regions注册进index。不要把AX e*引用、native视觉v*引用与sky整数索引当作同一命名空间。


# 先做这39项，再展开矩阵

不要按734条从头跑到尾。总数分为453条机械生成schema排列、113条逐项编写的契约场景、168个运行环境矩阵格。没有任何产品测试已运行或通过。先看主工程手册模块依赖，再做 `p0-critical-subset.json` 的39条静态可判定检查。

这39条按schema→transport→target/state→diff→images→approval→UI顺序排列。它们只需要原创建造的fake daemon、fake filesystem、fake HTTP和虚拟时钟，不能启动Alma。`check_p0_observations.py ACTUAL.json` 是可执行的判定器，不是产品实现或自动驱动器。它对真实实现的独立观测做严格投影比较；不能把expected文件复制成actual作为测试。

ACTUAL.json以case ID为键，每个值为预期结构对应的实际观测。额外未断言字段允许存在；所有预期字段必须存在；数组顺序和长度精确；boolean不能以0/1冒充；数值精确。几何输入选择了可精确表示的数值，P0不使用宽松浮点容差。完整OS截图的±1px规则另见README。

## 观测采集约定

- schema_validator：将input.args交给待实现schema，记录schema_valid；scope写固定文字以区分schema与行为，禁止调用handler
- daemon_client_fake_bytestream：先建立input.pending对应调用；推进输入字节、close和虚拟时钟。resolutions/rejections必须由promise回调记录；pending_after来自可观测诊断接口；重放次数由write spy计数
- MCP_handler_fake_daemon：按input.setup设置index/HTTP/mock响应；其他P0 case统一无playbook、focus权限健康、approval=true、mutation RPC ok={}、postshot RPC ok={}。启动一个handler，完整记录RPC payload、批准query、补图目标与调用次数。只比较明确字段，不把UI文本当接收成功
- sky与StateDiff纯函数：注入input.prior_refs或baseline；分别记录renderer文本/index array或diff实际输出。`disabled_resets` 必须连续调用同一diff实例，不能为第二次创建新实例
- MCP图片：fake readFile由fixture://映射读合成bytes；实际content block解码后投影为image_blocks、filesystem_read_count、JSON字段存在性及实际base64/MIME。不得补做JPEG合法性检查来“修正”compatibility
- approval：HTTP spy给指定status/body；gate进入内函数计dispatch_allowed；拒绝时检查内容有无isError和log/PiP调用。approved=true与truthy不等价
- UI：从空stack或case指定tombstone开始；只注入虚拟clock并触发指定事件。ring坐标由实际布局函数返回，不从expected复制
- null字段在这些投影中表示“不适用”，不是要求真实MCP新增null字段。例如审批允许时denial_log_and_pip_calls投影为null，避免把成功调用次数误作拒绝路径断言

前提/观测接口不满足时标NOT_RUN并补测试驱动，不修改expected来迁就实现。P0全部通过仍不能宣称后台操作、锁屏抓图或无焦点抢占成立；S3以后的受控macOS证据是这些能力的硬门槛。

P0新增5项：UTF-8字节内部拆分、超时迟到只本地拒绝且不重放、safe重连清buffer、safe未知结局、safe目标绑定。前三十二项为compatibility契约，最后五项中后三项明确为safe要求；它们不是safe产品发布的完整最小集合。发布仍按S3—S7能力与见证门槛。UTF-8用receive_chunks_hex逐段解码为bytes再喂framer，禁止每段先转Unicode字符串。超时测试只断言本地状态，不推断native副作用。

最后两项（现为39项）验证跨层v*可达性：普通click(ref=v1,bundle=fixture)不得被fake daemon直接假设成功，应接入native dispatch的resolveActionPid分支模拟，并在缺pid/bundle时报精确invalid_arg；visual cache的存在不能救活该请求。sky状态含visual_regions但elements为空时，索引必须仍为空。两项均是兼容负例，不意味着安全实现也应丢失目标。


# 附录 A 全部 MCP 工具逐字段契约
本附录为静态Zod契约的独立转录，不是运行tools/list抓包。未断言未知属性strip行为。必需字段在schema层校验；通过schema不代表handler必要条件、ref或OS动作成功。省略与null不同。英文键值保持机器契约原意；数值默认和转发优先级按每项单列。

## launch_app
native cmd：launch_app；应用gate：True；证据：MCP.tool.launch_app

| 字段 | 必需 | 类型与边界 | 包装默认 |
|---|---|---|---|
| bundle | 是 | string | 未单列 |
| activates | 否 | boolean | false |

逐字段转发与优先级：
- bundle：bundle
- activates：activates ?? false

返回约定：native-data-as-pretty-JSON-text
批准目标：bundle only (no PID/ref resolution)

## list_apps
native cmd：list_apps；应用gate：False；证据：MCP.tool.list_apps

| 字段 | 必需 | 类型与边界 | 包装默认 |
|---|---|---|---|
| days | 否 | integer; maximum=90 | 14 |

逐字段转发与优先级：
- days：positive integer days else 14

返回约定：native-data-as-pretty-JSON-text

## get_app_state
native cmd：get_app_state；应用gate：False；证据：MCP.tool.get_app_state

| 字段 | 必需 | 类型与边界 | 包装默认 |
|---|---|---|---|
| bundle | 否 | string | 未单列 |
| pid | 否 | integer | 未单列 |
| window_id | 否 | integer | 未单列 |
| depth | 否 | integer; maximum=20 | 6 |
| interactive_only | 否 | boolean | true |
| screenshot_max_width | 否 | integer | 1280 |
| auto_launch | 否 | boolean | true |

逐字段转发与优先级：
- bundle：raw bundle
- pid：positive integer pid else omitted
- window_id：positive window_id else omitted
- depth：nonnegative integer depth else 6
- interactive_only：arg ?? true
- include_screenshot：true
- screenshot_max_width：positive integer else 1280
- auto_launch：arg ?? true

返回约定：optional-playbook+JSON-without-screenshot+path-read-JPEG-image-or-text-warning
边角：
- Neither bundle nor pid is schema-required; daemon decides missing-target behavior.
- No disable_diff argument supplied.

## click
native cmd：click；应用gate：True；证据：MCP.tool.click

| 字段 | 必需 | 类型与边界 | 包装默认 |
|---|---|---|---|
| ref | 否 | string | 未单列 |
| x | 否 | number | 未单列 |
| y | 否 | number | 未单列 |
| coord_space | 否 | string; 枚举 screenshot, screen | screenshot |
| button | 否 | string; 枚举 left, right, middle | left |
| click_count | 否 | integer; maximum=3 | 1 |
| strategy | 否 | string; 枚举 auto, physical, ax | auto |
| bundle | 否 | string | 未单列 |
| pid | 否 | integer | 未单列 |
| window_id | 否 | integer | 未单列 |

逐字段转发与优先级：
- common：["button","positive click_count else 1","strategy","show_cursor=true"]
- ref_branch：["ref"]
- coordinate_branch：["x","y","coord_space","normalized pid","normalized bundle"]

返回约定：action-summary+conditional-image
批准目标：bundle only (no PID/ref resolution)
边角：
- Truthy ref takes precedence. Otherwise both x and y must exist.
- window_id is not sent to action; may be sent to post-action capture.

## 共用传输参数
- mcp：stdio JSON-RPC via MCP SDK
- native：Unix-domain stream; UTF-8 JSON lines
- request_fields：{"id":"random 8 bytes encoded as hex","cmd":"command string","args":"JSON object"}
- response_fields：{"id":"request correlation","ok":"truthy success test","data":"successful result","error":{"message":"string","code":"attached to JS Error","hint":"attached to JS Error"}}
- request_timeout_ms：20000
- ordinary_state_timeout_ms：30000
- post_action_state_timeout_ms：30000
- sky_state_timeout_ms：20000
- daemon_idle_seconds：900
- spawn_connect_retry_window_ms：4000
- retry_delay_ms：100
- timeout_starts：after ensureConnected resolves
- socket_scope：helper path string SHA1 first8, not binary hash
- action_auto_retry：false
- late_response：ignored after pending removal
- disconnect：reject every pending; next call reconnects
- cancel_forwarding：No AbortSignal or cancel command in application DaemonClient
- evidence：["MCP.transport","MCP.sdk-cancel","MCP.sdk-handler-abort","MCP.stdio"]

## 共用结果语义
- native_failure：SDK tool error text+isError:true; code/hint not emitted by ordinary error wrapper
- approval_denied：text content without isError:true
- key_suppressed：delivered=false => semantic not-delivered text, not isError
- key_menu_route：via=menu_ax_press => menu route text
- post_action_capture_failure：summary plus warning text, not action error
- ordinary_state_image：read screenshot.path, base64 image/jpeg block
- sky_state_image：data_url || url embedded inside JSON text, path alone ignored
- ref_only_capture：none unless caller separately supplies bundle or positive pid

# 附录 B 全部 CLI 命令逐字段契约
22个功能canonical命令，加help共23项；launch是launch_app别名，总24个可识别字符串。这里是JavaScript CLI，不是native helper的daemon/version/help启动CLI。

## 共用解析器
- base_url：ALMA_API_URL || http://localhost:23001
- valued_flags：--foo=value OR --foo value; scans full args, returns first matching value
- boolean_flags：exact --foo presence only; --foo=true does not count
- json_flag：Only commands explicitly branching asJson honor it; not a universal raw-output mode.
- numeric_coercion：parseInt/parseFloat may accept numeric prefix; most integer flag paths do not validate finite/range; JSON.stringify(NaN/Infinity) emits null.
- target：digits-only string becomes PID; any other nonempty string becomes bundle (negative PID-like string becomes bundle)
- pre_action_common_parser：After snap handling and before action selection, button is validated against left/right/middle, click_count parsed, showCursor=!no-cursor. Thus an invalid --button can fail even an unrelated later command.
- unknown_subcommand：stderr usage hint then exit1
- http：Content-Type application/json; body JSON.stringify when body argument is defined; no per-request AbortSignal/timeout; non2xx prints HTTP error and exit1; JSON decoded only if response content-type contains application/json; otherwise text; fetch exceptions print connection error and exit1
- semantic_success：HTTP2xx alone returns body; callsite determines meaning. Some callsites ignore body entirely.
- safety_note：This catalog describes quirks for compatibility testing, not a recommendation to copy misleading success or lax validation. Prefer explicit typed outcome in a new implementation.

# 附录 C 全部原生命令与可达面
当前ARM64 Daemon.dispatch的顶层cmd链共45项；23个MCP注册工具直达13个不同native cmd，其余32项不能当成23-tool MCP别名。命令存在性和全部参数/错误语义闭合是两种覆盖率，见后面的extra-command契约。

| 原生 cmd | 比较 VA | 成功入口 VA | 23 MCP可达 |
|---|---|---|---|
| ping | 0x100076a1c | 0x100076a24 | 否 |
| permissions | 0x100076bb8 | 0x100076bc0 | 是 |
| grant | 0x100076d8c | 0x100076d94 | 是 |
| apps | 0x100076e70 | 0x100076e78 | 否 |
| list_apps | 0x100076f84 | 0x100076f8c | 是 |
| windows | 0x10007703c | 0x100077044 | 否 |
| snap | 0x1000771e4 | 0x1000771ec | 否 |
| launch_app | 0x1000773f4 | 0x1000773fc | 是 |
| open_url | 0x1000775f4 | 0x1000775fc | 否 |
| intent | 0x100077888 | 0x100077890 | 否 |
| get_app_state | 0x100077ad8 | 0x100077ae0 | 是 |
| click | 0x100077f78 | 0x100077f80 | 是 |
| type | 0x10007847c | 0x100078484 | 否 |

## native CLI 与前置 gate
native CLI无参数默认为daemon；version/--version/-v输出版本0.1.0，独立于plist1.0.0和host0.4.164。help/--help/-h；未知subcommand退出2。daemon flags为--socket PATH、--idle-seconds N、--lens、--no-lens、--help、-h；没有--idle别名。未识别daemon flag忽略；缺operand不专门报错；idle完整Double parse成功才覆盖。实际CLI idle默认900s，help/initializer写600s；wrapper也显式900s。Lens初始false，最后--lens/--no-lens获胜。
共享preflight action set只有click、perform_secondary_action、drag、type、type_text、press、press_key、scroll、set_value，先更新Lens目标再ForbiddenTargets.check；另intent/open_url按bundle检查。select_text、launch_app、raise、remote_input不在这两组。只描述dispatcher级范围，不推断其余层没有gate。
native helper启动、socket路由、native MCPServer与JavaScript stdio MCP是不同命名空间，禁止混用。完整地址说明见analysis/native-snapshot/DISPATCH_INVENTORY.md。

# 附录 D 原生扩展命令精确契约
以下完整保留本研究独立撰写的功能契约，非Alma提取源码或反汇编。正文中文章解释主要实现路径；本附录保留字段拼写、原始技术措辞与精确地址，便于直接实现和逐项核验。data均位于标准wire envelope内部。工具名出现不代表默认产品路径使用，也不代表OS执行已验证。

## UTILITY COMMANDS
证据文件：analysis/native-snapshot/UTILITY_COMMANDS.md


Alma0.4.164 arm64 unslid static evidence. Result schemas below are the `data` inside WIRE_SCHEMA's envelope; errors use its nested error schema. This supplements45-command inventory. Inputs not listed are not parsed by the inspected command body. PIP/Appshot/remote commands have separate contracts; native MCP service is a separate subsystem.

### ping

Args none. Returns `{pong:true,version:"0.1.0",uptime_s:Int}`. uptime_s=Int(NSProcessInfo.processInfo.systemUptime), truncation toward zero; this is **OS uptime**, not elapsed daemon lifetime. Dispatch0x100076a24; version immediate0x100076a90–a98; clock0x100076ae0; cast0x100076b28. The helper protocol version0.1.0 is separate from app release0.4.164.

### permissions

Args none. Returns:

    {accessibility:Bool,screen_recording:Bool,
     focus_guard:{armed:Bool,arm_failures:Int}}

health tuple0x100076bcc; accessibility `isTrusted(prompt:false)`0x100076c38; screen capture permission probe0x100076c6c; nested health keys0x100076cb8–cf0. The SC permission probe uses ShareableContentCache via synchronous async bridge, so it is an actual permission check, not merely a stored preference. Missing permission is a false flag, not itself failure of this command.

### grant

Args none. Calls `isTrusted(prompt:true)`; returns `{prompted:true,accessibility:Bool}`. 0x100076d94–e18. `prompted:true` reports the requesting operation, not proof that permission was granted or UI appeared. Does not grant Screen Recording automatically.

### apps

Args none. Returns `{apps:[{bundle:String?,name:String,pid:Int32,active:Bool}]}`; mapper0x10007fa04 builds exactly four fields bundle/name/pid/active. Native AXBridge.runningApps0x10000fe34 selects data; null/absent bundle semantics follow optional dictionary values (canonical null). This is distinct from list_apps usage-history command. RunningApps selection/sort policy is closed in the follow-on subsection below; do not substitute list_apps.

### windows

Args `bundle?:String,pid?:Int32` using args coercions; same resolveApp PID-first/live-app fallback defined in WIRE_SCHEMA. No implicit frontmost.

Returns `{app:{bundle:String?,pid:Int32},windows:[{id:UInt32,title:String,bounds:{x,y,w,h},minimized:Bool,focused:Bool}]}`. App object contains bundle and pid, not localized name in this branch. Native calls resolveApp0x1000770d0 then AXBridge.windows0x1000770f0; wraps app/windows at0x100077130–74e8. Window mapper0x10007ff34–8027c. Windows retain AX source order; no sorting. Missing app -> appNotFound; AXWindows copy errors propagate via CUError; nil/unconvertible AXWindows yields empty array. See FINDINGS section10 for focused/geometry/ID defaults.

### snap

Literal `snap`, not `snapshot`. Args bundle?,pid?,window_id?,depth=6,interactive_only=true,wakeup_seconds=0. Returns full SnapshotResult dictionary and does not run AppStateDiff or capture. Exact schema/algorithms in FINDINGS sections2–4. This is optional direct-native functionality; current wrapper post-action uses get_app_state and therefore has different diff effects.

### raise

Args `bundle?:String,pid?:Int32,window_id?:UInt32`; checked conversions can trap if out of range. Uses resolveApp then AXBridge.windows. Window selection: explicit matching ID first, else focused first, else first AX window. Calls AXRaise on chosen AX element (0x10001683c); return checked through AX error path; success data `{raised:true}` (0x10007dbc4–be0). It is not guaranteed to activate the application or preserve user focus; AXRaise runtime behavior depends on the app.

Important: empty AX window array reaches BRK in selection0x100016918 rather than a demonstrated friendly no-window error. Hardened replica should return typed error, declaring this difference. Native symbol entry0x100016670; dispatcher0x10007db80.

### shot

Args:

- `out:String` required path; missing/empty/wrong type uses requireStr invalid_arg
- `max_width?:Int`, no native default (nil means no cap)
- `window_id?:UInt32`
- `bundle?:String,pid?:Int32`, used only when window_id absent

Algorithm: if window_id supplied, capture it directly without requiring PID/bundle; otherwise resolveApp and choose **first AX window**, not focused preference. If array empty, internal error message `App has no windows to capture.` (0x10007e810–830). Calls Capture.captureWindow ScreenCaptureKit via runAsync0x10007e508, quality fixed0.85. Failure propagates native failure; no AX-success/screenshot_error wrapper unlike get_app_state.

Result `{path:String,width:Int,height:Int,cursor?:{x:Double,y:Double}}`. Capture image always writes supplied path. Optional cursor is normalized0..1 logical ActionCursor position within **live CG window bounds**; omit when window bounds absent, no current ActionCursor, nonpositive rect size, or cursor outside rect. It is metadata, not a burned-in cursor. Base fields0x10007e8cc–91c; windowBounds0x10007e93c; ActionCursor.normalized0x10007e95c; cursor insertion0x10007e9fc. Successful shot does **not** update Daemon.lastMapping or visualRefs in this dispatch path. Those caches belong to get_app_state screenshot flow.

### shot_display

Args `out:String` required, `max_width?:Int` defaultnil, `display_id?:UInt32` defaultnil, `quality?:Double` default0.6 (IEEE7540x3fe3333333333333 at0x10007dcc4–d0).

Calls Capture.captureDisplay via runAsync0x10007dd18. Result same `{path,width,height,cursor?}`. For cursor metadata uses supplied display ID if present, else CGMainDisplayID, then CGDisplayBounds and ActionCursor.normalized0x10007e058–06c. Underlying SC display selector uses ShareableContentCache display TTL30; image configuration differences vs window capture are detailed in the follow-on subsection below. No AX snapshot, diff or mapping update. Invalid requested display/missing permissions/failing write propagate errors.

### ActionCursor normalization (shared shot/shot_display)

0x10002a0e4: get current logical action cursor; require positive rect width+height and CGRectContainsPoint; x=(point.x-rect.minX)/rect.width,y=(point.y-rect.minY)/rect.height. No clamping to boundary or conversion to screenshot pixels. ActionCursor lifetime itself is described by native input report. This is not necessarily hardware mouse position.

### Nonexistent requested names

No top-level `status` or `frontmost` command in Daemon.dispatch45 inventory. Relevant actual commands are ping/permissions, pip_status/mcp_status, and appshot_frontmost. Do not add aliases while claiming protocol parity.

### Scope boundaries

These contracts close host utility **wire fields** and main control flow; native subprocess/OS scheduling, app-provided AX behavior, SC display capture specifics and private APIs still require capability tests. A host mock can implement the above shapes now; production parity should gate capture until permitted macOS execution verifies dimensions and permissions. Never infer exposure through JS MCP23 from a socket handler's existence.

#### Follow-on static closure: apps selection and display capture

`apps` selects NSWorkspace.runningApplications with activationPolicy==0/regular (0x10000ff68–6c), builds bundle/name/pid, uses `(unknown)` when localizedName absent (0x1000100f8–10c), marks active by PID equality with initially sampled frontmostApplication (0x10000fe74 and0x100010134–13c), then sorts case-insensitively by lowercased names (comparison0x1000176ac–6ec). Thus it is ordinary GUI apps, not all running processes. Equal-key ordering follows the inspected stable sort implementation.

`shot_display` Capture.captureDisplay0x10003feb8:

- ShareableContentCache.display(id) gets fresh(maxAge30), chooses matching explicit display ID or first display when absent, invalidates/retries once on no match, then throws if still missing. Picker0x100040c18–0x100040de4; unlike cursor metadata's absent-ID branch, the capture picker does not itself call CGMainDisplayID.
- SCContentFilter(display,excludingWindows:[])0x100040064
- Find NSScreen by deviceDescription['NSScreenNumber'] matching selected SCDisplay.displayID (0x100040198–244); use matched screen backingScaleFactor, else mainScreen scale, else2.0 (0x1000402e8–314)
- width=Int(SCDisplay.width*scale), height=Int(SCDisplay.height*scale); positive smaller max_width downscales proportionally with minimum height1, using truncation
- **showsCursor=true**0x100040464–468 (window capture uses false); capturesAudio=false; minimumFrameInterval1/60
- macOS>=14 SCScreenshotManager.captureImage; older branch internalError0x10004058c; writeJPEG requested quality, return actual image pixel width/height

The metadata cursor still refers to ActionCursor and may coexist with ScreenCaptureKit's actual system cursor pixels. Absent display_id may imply SC first-display capture while cursor normalization uses CGMainDisplayID; preserve and test that distinction instead of silently assuming identical selection.

### open_url (optional native extension)

Args `url:String` required nonempty; `bundle?:String` preferred opener. No PID targeting in body. Parse with Foundation.URL(string:), invalid parse => invalid_arg `invalid url: <supplied text>` (0x10007774c–77f4). Success calls AppCommandRegistry.openQuietly(url,preferredBundleId)0x100077a24 and returns `{opened:<original supplied URL String>,via:<supplied bundle String or null>}` (0x100077b64–7ef0). It does not return `{opened:true}`.

openQuietly0x100031414 creates NSWorkspace.OpenConfiguration with activates=false, addsToRecentItems=false, hidesOthers=false (0x100031528–54c). Preferred bundle resolves its application URL; when usable calls openURLs:[url] withApplicationAtURL; otherwise general openURL:configuration. Uses completion+semaphore wait with5s budget. Timeout -> internal `NSWorkspace.open(<absoluteString>) timed out after 5s`; callback error -> internal `NSWorkspace.open(<absoluteString>) failed: <localizedDescription>` (0x100031994–19ec,31a84–31b1c). The5s error does not itself establish that OS launch/open was cancelled.

Both open_url and intent pass bundleId through ForbiddenTargets dispatcher preflight. This establishes quiet-intent configuration, not a universal OS guarantee of zero frontmost changes.

### intent (optional native registry extension)

Args required nonempty `bundle:String,intent:String`; optional `params:object` default empty. Nonobject params casts unsuccessfully and is treated as empty. Coerce each nonnull params value to String in order String / Int.description / Double.description / Bool=>"true"|"false" / String(describing:value); null is skipped. Evidence params lookup0x100077be4; dict cast0x1000782d4; scalar branches0x1000785dc,78610,7865c,78690; fallback0x100078720.

Calls AppCommandRegistry.dispatch(bundleId,intent,params)0x100077c4c. On success returns `{bundle:<requested>,intent:<requested>,url:<constructed URL.absoluteString>}`0x1000781e0–2270. Registry rejects unknown bundle, unknown intent (includes known-intent list), missing required params, and invalid constructed URL through invalid_arg paths0x100030cc0/31000/31158/30e78; successful built URL is passed to openQuietly0x100031090.

**Scope classification:** no JS MCP23 tool maps to open_url or intent. Their socket schemas are closed above. Registry metadata emitted by core list_apps is reconstructed in APP_INTENTS_METADATA.md; executable URL-builder behavior remains outside this supplement. Treat that registry as an optional native extension, not a implemented core capability. A clone may explicitly return unsupported for this optional extension until it supplies the same registry, but must not claim45-command behavioral parity. Current evidence of default desktop control is the separate JS stdio MCP adapter; use host/mirror reports to decide which administrative/native extensions to include.

## APP USAGE CONTRACT
证据文件：analysis/native-snapshot/APP_USAGE_CONTRACT.md


### Scope and primary evidence

Static inspection only. No target executable was run, no Spotlight query was issued, and no live application/history data was read. Addresses below are **unslid arm64 virtual addresses**, not file offsets. Primary artifacts: `analysis/native/arm64.macho`, `arm64.json`, `arm64-symbols.txt`, and `arm64-disasm.txt`; ranges can be reproduced by disassembling the same-hash independently acquired sample at the listed VAs; complete raw dumps and vendor binaries are not distributed; this consolidated package separately includes bounded, provenance-labeled .153 disassembly excerpts and task-specific JS snippets under code-evidence. JavaScript source is the shipped `extracted/Alma.app/Contents/Resources/cli/alma-computer-use-mcp.mjs`.

This closes usage-window handling, source/query lifecycle, running-app selection, merging, duplicate behavior, fallback values, final comparator, and emitted row shape. Registry metadata exposed by `intents` is specified in the companion `APP_INTENTS_METADATA.md`; URL builders are a separate native extension.

### 1. Entry points and days

- Public MCP `list_apps`: source lines 36007–36024. Input `days` is optional Zod number, integer, maximum 90, with **no schema minimum**. The handler sends `{days: posIntOr(args.days,14)}`; `posIntOr` requires a finite numeric value greater than zero, then floors it, otherwise returns 14. Consequently valid MCP positive values are integers 1–90, while zero and negative integers are accepted by the schema but become 14. Missing becomes 14; fractions and values above 90 fail schema validation rather than being clamped. No interaction gate wraps this tool. The result is one text content block containing `JSON.stringify(res,null,2)`.
- Native dispatcher branch `0x100076f8c–0x100077004`: reads `days` using the native optional-dictionary `int` accessor at `0x100076fa4`; missing/null value or an accessor parse failure gets literal 14 via `mov w8,#0xe` / `csel x0,x8,x0,eq` at `0x100076fb0–fb4`; calls `AppUsage.listApps(days:)` at `0x100076fb8`.
- Swift default-argument generator `0x10003791c`: `mov w0,#0xe; ret`.
- The native `int` accessor also accepts Double (truncate toward zero) and signed decimal String, after trying Int (`0x100006308–6340`, `0x100006388–6400`, `0x100006404–66bc`; see `WIRE_SCHEMA.md`). Non-finite/out-of-Int-range Double conversion traps at `0x1000066c8–66d0`; that is not the default14 fallback. Ordinary MCP schema validation excludes these non-integer/non-number inputs before native forwarding.
- Native AppUsage has **no positivity or maximum clamp**. It passes the original integer to `recentMetadata` at `0x1000384e8–4f0`. `recentMetadata` holds the argument in x20 from `0x10003b720`; at `0x10003b994–9b8` it constructs Date(), converts x20 with `scvtf d0,x20`, multiplies by IEEE754 bits `0xc0f5180000000000` (= -86400.0), and calls `Date.addingTimeInterval`. Native direct callers can therefore request zero, negative, and >90 windows. Do not incorrectly impose the MCP restriction on the raw daemon contract.
- Public `sky_list_apps`, lines 36294–36310, sends native `list_apps` with `{}`, so native default 14 applies. It returns a JSON text **array** transformed from `res.apps ?? []`: `id = a.bundle || a.name || "unknown"`, `displayName = a.name`, `isRunning = a.running ?? a.active`, `lastUsedDate = a.last_used_at ?? undefined`, `useCount = a.use_count ?? undefined`. Missing/null lastUsedDate/useCount are omitted by JSON.stringify. This differs from the ordinary tool's full native result object.

### 2. Native result shape

Native result is `{apps: [row, ...]}`. Dispatcher builds the `apps` dictionary, with the mapped array at `0x100076ff0–0x100077000` and shared dictionary-return tail `0x100076ec4–0x100076f18`. Each row has **nine** keys, built by mapper `0x100080884–0x1000810a8`:

| JSON key | Type / value | Primary instruction proof |
|---|---|---|
| bundle | String or null | Small-string literal at `0x100080978–994`; AppInfo+0 and +8 read at `0x100080924`; optional nil branch `0x1000809ac–9b8` |
| name | String | Literal `0x1000809bc–9d0`; AppInfo+0x10/+0x18 stored `0x1000809d4–9d8` |
| pid | Int32 promoted to signed integer, or null | Literal `0x1000809dc–9f4`; optional tag at +0x24; `ldrsw` from +0x20 at `0x100080a14` |
| active | Bool | Literal `0x100080a24–a38`; value from AppInfo+0x25 at `0x100080a3c–a48` |
| running | Bool | Literal `0x100080a4c–a64`; value from AppInfo+0x26 at `0x100080a68–a6c` |
| path | String or null | Literal `0x100080a70–a80`; AppInfo+0x28/+0x30, optional test `0x100080a84–ab0` |
| use_count | Swift Int or null | Literal `0x100080ab4–ad0`; value +0x38, optional tag +0x40 at `0x100080ad4–afc` |
| last_used_at | ISO8601 String or null | Literal `0x100080b00–b20`; optional Date copied/tested `0x100080b24–b64`; nil existential is zeroed `0x100080b68–b6c`; nonnil formatting `0x100080ba0–c28` |
| intents | Array of objects, possibly [] | Literal `0x100080c3c–c50`; exact bundle lookup `0x100080930–940`; nil bundle gets empty array at `0x100080948`; empty registry array branch `0x10008101c–1028`; emitted array stored `0x100081040–1048` |

The mapper destroys exactly nine key/value literal entries at `0x10008107c` (`mov w1,#9`) after building `[String:Any?]` at `0x100081050`. Optional fields remain present as null in the canonical native JSON conversion; they are not all omitted like the sky adapter's optional date/count fields.

`last_used_at` uses a newly initialized `NSISO8601DateFormatter` for each present date, then `stringFromDate:`; no formatter options, timezone, fractional-seconds option, or custom format is configured in the mapper. This is Foundation's default ISO8601 formatter behavior. Classref `0x1000ae488` contains raw chained-bind word `0x80100000000002c9`; fixups import index 0x2c9 resolves `_OBJC_CLASS_$_NSISO8601DateFormatter`. Do not substitute Unix epoch numbers or a locale-dependent date formatter.

Each intent has precisely `name:String`, `description:String`, `required_params:[String]`, `optional_params:[String]`; key/value materialization `0x100080cb0–0x100080d50`, dictionary capacity four at `0x100080d54`, array append `0x100080fd4–fe8`. No URL/build closure is emitted. Registry order is preserved by this map. Exact entries are in the companion document.

### 3. Running-app seed

1. Start a `[String:AppInfo]` dictionary and a separate array for rows without bundle IDs (`0x100037cb4–cc4`, `0x100037de4–dec`).
2. Read `NSWorkspace.shared.frontmostApplication`; capture its PID if present (`0x100037ccc–0x100037d30`). Retain a separate presence test, so absence does not make PID 0 active.
3. Read `NSWorkspace.shared.runningApplications` (`0x100037d34–d80`) and enumerate its order (`0x100037e18–e58`).
4. **Include only activationPolicy == 0 (`.regular`)**. Call and `cbnz` skip are `0x100037e5c–e64`. Accessory/prohibited/background apps are excluded from the running seed; the metadata stage is not filtered by activation policy.
5. For each included application:
   - `bundleId = bundleIdentifier` optional (`0x100037e68–ea0`)
   - `name = localizedName ?? "(unknown)"` (`0x100037eac–ef0`); the fallback is the exact lower-case, parenthesized string decoded from immediates 0x6e776f6e6b6e7528 + 0xe900000000000029
   - `pid = processIdentifier` (`0x100037ef8–efc`)
   - `active = frontmost exists && processIdentifier == frontmostPID` (`0x100037f04–f24`)
   - `running = true` (`0x100038068–06c`)
   - `path = bundleURL?.path`, not executableURL (`0x100037f30`, `0x100037ff4`); no URL -> nil (`0x100037fd0–fec`)
   - `useCount = nil`, `lastUsedAt = nil` (`0x10003801c–03c`, `0x100038074–078`)
6. Non-nil bundle IDs are dictionary keys with normal exact Swift String equality. A later running application with the same bundle replaces the entire earlier AppInfo (`find` `0x1000382a8`; found branch `0x100038388–3a4`; insertion `0x1000383d0–444`). No PID/path disambiguation and no explicit case folding are used.
7. Nil bundle rows append separately (`0x1000381e4–264`). They are not deduplicated by name, path, or PID.

### 4. Spotlight history source and query lifecycle

`AppUsage.recentMetadata(days:)` is `0x10003b6fc–0x10003c750`. The source is **NSMetadataQuery**, not Screen Time, an Alma-maintained database, launch logs, recent-document menus, or a recursive filesystem scan.

#### Configuration

- Allocate/init NSMetadataQuery at `0x10003b8f4–b904`. Classref `0x1000ae508` raw bind word `0x80100000000002d9`, fixups import 0x2d9 = `_OBJC_CLASS_$_NSMetadataQuery`.
- Set search scopes to a one-element array containing **NSMetadataQueryLocalComputerScope** (`0x10003b940–988`). GOT slot `0x1000a4800` resolves that exact symbol using LC_DYSYMTAB indirect symbols; raw bind word `0x80100000000000f8`.
- Cutoff: current Date plus `-86400.0 * Double(days)` as above. The comparison is inclusive.
- Predicate format at raw C-string VA `0x100093240`: `kMDItemContentType == 'com.apple.application-bundle' AND kMDItemLastUsedDate >= %@`. Construct NSPredicate with the cutoff NSDate at `0x10003b9fc–ba9c`. Equality on content type is exact; this is not a ContentTypeTree/conformance test.
- `valueListAttributes` = `["kMDItemDisplayName", "kMDItemCFBundleIdentifier", "kMDItemLastUsedDate", "kMDItemUseCount", "kMDItemPath"]`. Static Swift array begins VA `0x1000b0730`, count 5 at +0x10, capacity word 10 at +0x18, elements at +0x20. Its large strings point to C-string VAs `0x1000932a0`, `0x1000932c0`, `0x1000932e0`; the last two are inline Swift small strings at `0x1000b0780`, `0x1000b0790`. Array selected at `0x10003babc–bac4`, passed to `setValueListAttributes:` at `0x10003bae8`.
- Query sort descriptor is `NSSortDescriptor(key:"kMDItemLastUsedDate",ascending:false)` (`0x10003bb38–bb60`; w3=0); installed at `0x10003bba8`. Thus metadata is traversed in descending last-used order before the independent final AppInfo sort.

#### Execution and fallback

- Create semaphore value 0 at `0x10003bbb4–bbb8`.
- Add default NotificationCenter observer for **NSMetadataQueryDidFinishGatheringNotification**, scoped to this query object, with queue nil (`0x10003bbe4–bbec`, `0x10003bc74–bc88`). Exact notification GOT slot `0x1000a47f8` resolves the named symbol, raw bind word `0x80100000000000f7`.
- Observer closure `0x10003da34–da50` only signals the semaphore (`0x10003da44`).
- Dispatch `startQuery` asynchronously to the main queue (`0x10003bce4`, `0x10003bde8`); closure `0x10003da70–da74` tail-calls `startQuery`. The Bool return of startQuery is not used to produce an error.
- Wait until `DispatchTime.now() + 2.0` (`0x10003be44–be58`, literal `fmov d0,#2`), semaphore wait `0x10003be80`; compare return against timeout (`0x10003be94–bea0`).
- Enqueue `stopQuery` asynchronously on main queue in either case (`0x10003beac`, `0x10003bf78`; closure `0x10003db0c–db10`). Then remove the observer (`0x10003bfd4`). There is no `disableUpdates` call and no synchronous main-queue stop before result extraction.
- **On timeout return []**, discarding any partial query results (`0x10003bfe8–c014`). Consequently list_apps falls back to its regular running-app seed and still returns normal success; it does not expose a timeout error or partial-history flag. This is not a fallback to another usage database.
- On successful gathering, read `resultCount` and iterate `resultAtIndex:` (`0x10003c018–c028`, `0x10003c110–c160`). Rows failing the conditional NSMetadataItem cast are skipped. Zero results returns [].

#### Extracted metadata rows

For each NSMetadataItem, build MDEntry with:

| MDEntry member | Value and fallback | Primary range |
|---|---|---|
| bundleId | `valueForAttribute("kMDItemCFBundleIdentifier") as? String`, nil on absent/cast failure | `0x10003c164–c228` |
| name | `(kMDItemDisplayName as? String) ?? (kMDItemFSName as? String) ?? "(unknown)"` | `0x10003c228–c3a8`; FSName immediate construction `0x10003c2d0–2ec` |
| path | `(kMDItemPath as? String) ?? ""` | `0x10003c3ac–c468`; fallback x24=0,x19=0xe000000000000000 at `0x10003c460–464` |
| useCount | `kMDItemUseCount as? Int`, nil on absent/cast failure | `0x10003c468–c538`; destination Swift Int metadata GOT `0x1000a4a70` |
| lastUsedAt | `kMDItemLastUsedDate as? Date`, nil on absent/cast failure | `0x10003c538–c628` |

The final MDEntry write is `0x10003c658–c670`; append preserves query order (`0x10003c694–c6c8`, `0x10003c0a0–c110`). No `.app` suffix is trimmed from names; empty strings are preserved rather than treated as missing. FSName is read as an on-demand fallback even though it is not in valueListAttributes.

### 5. Exact merge and duplicate behavior

After collecting running apps, call recentMetadata once (`0x1000384e8–4f0`) and traverse its rows in returned order (`0x100038508–5e8`). For each metadata entry `m`:

- If m.bundleId exists and currently matches a dictionary row:
  - Preserve that row's bundle, name, pid, active, running (`0x10003866c–6a4` saves fields; `0x10003871c–754` restores them)
  - Preserve its path **if non-nil**; otherwise use m.path (`0x1000386a8–6e0`; `cbnz x26` skips fallback). Empty path counts as non-nil and is not subsequently improved
  - Replace useCount and lastUsedAt with m's optional values **unconditionally**, including nil (`0x1000386e4–718`, `0x100038758–760`)
  - Write the updated row back to the dictionary under that bundle (`0x100038c64–d80`)
- Otherwise make an AppInfo from metadata: bundle/name/path/useCount/date from m, pid=nil, active=false, running=false (`0x1000388ac–914`, notably `strh w8,[x24,#0x24]` with w8=1 encodes optional PID nil plus active false, and `strb wzr,[x24,#0x26]` running false)
  - With a non-nil bundle, insert it into the same dictionary (`0x10003891c–920`, `0x100038b04–c5c`)
  - With nil bundle, append to the separate unbundled-row array (`0x100038a68–afc`)

Important reproducibility edge cases:

1. Matching is **bundle-only**. There is no path/name matching for an unbundled app. A running unbundled row and metadata unbundled row for the same application remain two entries.
2. All bundled metadata rows deduplicate into the dictionary, even if none was originally running. Multiple installations with the same bundle ID become one row.
3. For duplicate metadata bundle IDs, the **first/newest** metadata row provides the name/path of a non-running row, but **each later/older row overwrites count/date**. Since query order is lastUsed descending, the last matching metadata row determines final use_count and last_used_at. Counts are not summed, maximized, or tied to the retained path. No newest-wins test appears in the merge path.
4. A previously running row's name/PID/flags remain the running row's values, even when a newer history entry exists. Dictionary replacement among running instances occurs before history merging, so the last running instance enumerated supplies them.
5. Unbundled rows are never deduplicated, neither running vs history nor metadata vs metadata.

Before final sort, form `Array(dictionary.values)` (`0x100038eb4–f18`, including concrete Dictionary.Values copy helper at `0x100038ef4`), then append the unbundled array (`0x100038f20–f30`). Therefore a fully tied pair of bundled rows has dictionary-enumeration order, not a promised deterministic insertion order. Unbundled rows enter after all dictionary rows, with their original running-then-metadata traversal order.

### 6. Final sort comparator, fully closed

`0x100038f4c` calls specialized sort `0x100039140`. It chooses insertion sort for short runs (`0x1000391dc–1ec`) and `_stableSortImpl` for larger arrays (`0x100039220–224`). The clearest inlined comparator is in insertion sort `0x100039dc0–e88`:

```
if lhs.active != rhs.active: return lhs.active
if lhs.running != rhs.running: return lhs.running
let l = lhs.useCount ?? -1
let r = rhs.useCount ?? -1
if l != r: return l > r
return lhs.name.lowercased() < rhs.name.lowercased()
```

Proof:
- active byte +0x25 comparison `0x100039dc0–dcc`; unequal branches with lhs flag in w22
- running byte +0x26 comparison `0x100039dd0–ddc`; same behavior
- lhs optional count tag +0x40 and count +0x38 at `0x100039de0–dec`; `csinv x8,x8,xzr,ne` produces -1 when nil; rhs analogous `0x100039df0–e14`
- descending signed count: `cmp x9,x8; cset w22,lt` at `0x100039e84–e88`
- lowercasing both names `0x100039e18–e2c`, strict Swift string-less comparison (`w4=1`) `0x100039e50–e60`
- equality returns no swap (`0x100039e3c–e4c` to `0x100039d48`), preserving stable input order for equal keys

**last_used_at is not used by the final comparator.** It filters/sorts the Spotlight input only. There is no bundle, PID, path, or raw-case name secondary tiebreaker. A negative count below -1 can sort behind a nil count because nil is explicitly -1, not a separate missing-value class. Replicas should reproduce Swift lowercased/string comparison semantics rather than locale-aware display-name sorting.

### 7. Minimal implementation sequence and fixture checks

Implement the running snapshot first, query with the specified cutoff/2-second lifecycle, merge as described, combine dictionary values and unbundled rows, stable-sort with the exact four-stage comparator, then map all nine fields and wrap in `apps`.

Static-derived conformance cases (synthetic inputs only):
- MCP missing/0/-2 -> sends14;1/90 preserved;91/fraction fail validation; raw native days0/-2/91 are not clamped
- accessory running app absent from seed; eligible history can still add it as running=false
- nil localizedName -> `(unknown)`; empty localizedName remains empty
- missing metadata display name -> FSName -> `(unknown)`; missing metadata path -> empty String
- same bundle in two running instances -> later instance wins; same bundle then two metadata rows -> later metadata count/date wins, running name/PID/path remain
- nil bundle twice -> two rows, no identity synthesis
- active outranks running/count; running outranks count; count descending with nil=-1; date never breaks ties; lowercased name ascending
- metadata timeout -> running-only successful `{apps:[...]}`
- ordinary list_apps emits null optionals and intents arrays; sky_list_apps emits transformed array, omitting null date/count and all intents

## APP INTENTS METADATA
证据文件：analysis/native-snapshot/APP_INTENTS_METADATA.md


### Scope

This is the built-in metadata exposed on ordinary MCP `list_apps` rows as `intents`. It is part of the default listing surface even though the raw daemon `intent` execution command and its URL-building implementations are a separate optional extension. This document closes exact bundle keys, ordered intent names, and ordered required/optional parameter names. Human-readable descriptions below are **paraphrases, not byte-exact description text**. Long original descriptions are not reproduced. `APP_USAGE_CONTRACT.md` specifies row selection/merge/sort and full output shape.

Primary evidence is the static initializer `AppCommandRegistry.entries` at **0x10002f184–0x10002fc08**, lookup `intents(for:)` at **0x1000309d4–0x100030a80**, the mapper **0x100080884–0x1000810a8**, and raw data in `analysis/native/arm64.macho`. All VAs are unslid arm64. No executable or registered intent was run.

### Lookup and emitted schema

- Mapper calls `intents(for: bundleId)` only for non-nil bundle (`0x100080924–940`). Nil bundle produces [] at `0x100080948`.
- Registry is lazy initialized once (`0x1000309ec–9f8`, `0x100030a68–a78`) and held at `0x1000b38a0`. Exact Swift String dictionary lookup at `0x100030a14–a20`: no lowercasing, suffix matching, identifier aliasing, wildcard, or fallback by name/path. An unregistered bundle yields [] (`0x100030a44–a48`).
- The initialized dictionary has exactly **7 keys**; capacity7 at `0x10002f884`, seven inserts `0x10002f89c–fb5c`, seven literal tuples destroyed at `0x10002fb90`. They contain **15 total intents** in the per-app order below. No system introspection or installed-app API contributes new intent metadata.
- Output for each intent: `{name:String, description:String, required_params:[String], optional_params:[String]}`. The mapper copies all four values; it does not emit the stored URL builder closure. Four dictionary literal values are built `0x100080cb0–d50`; capacity4 `0x100080d54`; order-preserving append loop `0x100080fd4–fe8`.
- These arrays are metadata listing parameter **names**, not per-parameter typed JSON schemas. Do not manufacture enum schemas or claim the descriptions enforce additional validations. Full URL-building behavior is outside this closure.
- `sky_list_apps` drops the entire `intents` field in its adapter mapping.

### Raw parameter-array proof

Each static Swift array's `UInt64` count is at base+0x10; elements are16-byte String records from base+0x20. These elements use inline small-string representation: first bytes are UTF-8 characters, top byte of second word is0xe0|length. Primary raw records:

| Static base VA | Count | Element values, in order | Small-string length tags |
|---|---:|---|---|
| 0x1000b0128 | 1 | song_id | e7 |
| 0x1000b0160 | 1 | playlist_id | eb |
| 0x1000b0198 | 1 | route | e5 |
| 0x1000b01d0 | 1 | url | e3 |
| 0x1000b0208 | 1 | uri | e3 |
| 0x1000b0240 | 5 | to, subject, body, cc, bcc | e2,e7,e4,e2,e3 |
| 0x1000b02b8 | 1 | url | e3 |
| 0x1000b02f0 | 2 | team_id, channel_id | e7,ea |

Empty arrays use x20, initialized by loading GOT **0x1000a4e88** at `0x10002f268–26c`; indirect-symbol table identifies **__swiftEmptyArrayStorage**. Raw chained-bind slot value is `0x80100000000001c9`. Thus the empty parameter lists above are proven empty arrays, not unresolved registers or omitted values.

- Arbitrary or nil bundle -> []

- Mail compose has zero required params and exactly5 optional params in `to,subject,body,cc,bcc` order
- Slack open_channel requires both team_id and channel_id in that order
- No description-summary column in this document should be mistaken for the original emitted description string; replicas may preserve functional metadata while explicitly excluding byte-identical prose from parity claims

## FORBIDDEN TARGETS CONTRACT

Sample0.4.164 arm64, no target execution and no live user data read. All claims A: instruction branches and literal data inspected. This records original behavior, not authorization to override safety controls in any assistant or deployment.

### Exact deny predicate

`ForbiddenTargets.isForbidden(bundleId:)`0x100032250 returns false for nil; otherwise Swift Set<String>.contains against a static18-entry set. There is no lowercasing, prefix/wildcard matching, executable-path check, signing team check, localized app-name match, or semantic app-category classifier in this predicate.

Set initializer0x100032050 allocates18 capacity (0x100032088), reads18 Swift string pairs beginning0x1000b03e0 (base0x1000b03b8+0x28, read0x1000320c8), loops exactly18 (0x1000320bc), and stores set0x1000b1f90. Exact case-preserving strings:

1. com.apple.systempreferences
2. com.apple.security.pluginkit
3. com.apple.security-notification
4. com.apple.preference.general
5. com.apple.loginwindow
